# Professional Agent Library

This extension adds 1,923 profession-specific agents and companion skills to the upstream Agency Agents roster.

Use an upstream specialist for a tool- or platform-specific assignment; use a profession agent for the wider professional role. Combine their outputs when both scopes are useful. Names are separated by the `Profession:` namespace.

The `professions/` division contains standalone native agent files. `extensions/professions/skills/` contains their companion skills. Folders use English profession names and a stable identifier. Runtime instructions incorporate expertise directly and contain no construction-source references.

Original reference materials, mapping decisions, translations and attribution are retained separately under `extensions/professions/audit/`, `sources/` and `data/`. Translations are machine-assisted and should be reviewed for high-stakes terminology. Original source documents are retained in their original language.

Rebuild the native division after editing the extension:

```bash
python scripts/integrate-professions.py --source extensions/professions
python scripts/validate-professions.py
```

The structured profession corpus shares workflow patterns across related roles. Its dedicated checks validate complete coverage, distinct identifiers, source preservation, skill structure and removal of provenance from runtime instructions. The upstream originality check continues to apply to the original editorial divisions.
